window.StudioEditor = {
    tbEditor: null,
    rtlEditor: null,
    projectFiles: {
        "testbench.sv": `module tb_counter;
    logic clk;
    logic rst_n;
    logic [3:0] d;
    logic [3:0] q;

    counter u_dut (.clk(clk), .rst_n(rst_n), .d(d), .q(q));

    initial begin
        $dumpfile("counter.vcd");
        $dumpvars;
        clk = 0; rst_n = 0; d = 4'd2;
        #5 rst_n = 1;
        #5 clk = 1; #5 clk = 0;
        #5 clk = 1; #5 clk = 0;
        #5 $finish;
    end
endmodule`,
        "design.sv": `module counter (
    input  logic       clk,
    input  logic       rst_n,
    input  logic [3:0] d,
    output logic [3:0] q
);

always_ff @(posedge clk or negedge rst_n) begin
    if (!rst_n) begin
        q <= 0;
    end else begin
        q <= q + d;
    end
end
endmodule`
    },
    activeTbFile: "testbench.sv",
    activeRtlFile: "design.sv",
    tbDecorations: [],
    rtlDecorations: [],

    init() {
        require.config({ paths: { 'vs': 'https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/monaco-editor/0.44.0/min/vs' }});
        require(['vs/editor/editor.main'], () => {
            monaco.editor.defineTheme('cas-dark', {
                base: 'vs-dark',
                inherit: true,
                rules: [
                    { token: 'keyword', foreground: '569cd6', fontStyle: 'bold' },
                    { token: 'identifier', foreground: '9cdcfe' },
                    { token: 'number', foreground: 'b5cea8' },
                    { token: 'string', foreground: 'ce9178' },
                    { token: 'comment', foreground: '6a9955', fontStyle: 'italic' }
                ],
                colors: {
                    'editor.background': '#131418',
                    'editor.lineHighlightBackground': '#1a1c22',
                    'editorCursor.foreground': '#007acc'
                }
            });

            this.tbEditor = monaco.editor.create(document.getElementById('editor-tb'), {
                value: this.projectFiles[this.activeTbFile],
                language: 'systemverilog',
                theme: 'cas-dark',
                fontSize: 13,
                fontFamily: 'JetBrains Mono',
                automaticLayout: true,
                minimap: { enabled: false }
            });

            this.rtlEditor = monaco.editor.create(document.getElementById('editor-rtl'), {
                value: this.projectFiles[this.activeRtlFile],
                language: 'systemverilog',
                theme: 'cas-dark',
                fontSize: 13,
                fontFamily: 'JetBrains Mono',
                automaticLayout: true,
                minimap: { enabled: false }
            });

            this.tbEditor.onDidChangeModelContent(() => {
                this.projectFiles[this.activeTbFile] = this.tbEditor.getValue();
            });

            this.rtlEditor.onDidChangeModelContent(() => {
                this.projectFiles[this.activeRtlFile] = this.rtlEditor.getValue();
            });

            this.renderTabs();
        });
    },

    renderTabs() {
        const tbContainer = document.getElementById('tb-tabs-list');
        const rtlContainer = document.getElementById('rtl-tabs-list');
        tbContainer.innerHTML = '';
        rtlContainer.innerHTML = '';

        for (const fname in this.projectFiles) {
            const isTbActive = (fname === this.activeTbFile);
            const isRtlActive = (fname === this.activeRtlFile);

            const isTbFile = fname.startsWith("tb_") || fname.startsWith("testbench");
            const targetContainer = isTbFile ? tbContainer : rtlContainer;
            const isActive = isTbFile ? isTbActive : isRtlActive;

            const tab = document.createElement('div');
            tab.className = 'editor-tab' + (isActive ? ' active' : '');
            
            const titleSpan = document.createElement('span');
            titleSpan.textContent = fname;
            titleSpan.onclick = () => this.switchTab(fname, isTbFile ? 'tb' : 'rtl');
            tab.appendChild(titleSpan);

            if (fname !== "testbench.sv" && fname !== "design.sv") {
                const closeBtn = document.createElement('span');
                closeBtn.className = 'tab-close-btn';
                closeBtn.textContent = '×';
                closeBtn.title = "Delete file";
                closeBtn.onclick = (e) => {
                    e.stopPropagation();
                    this.deleteFile(fname, isTbFile ? 'tb' : 'rtl');
                };
                tab.appendChild(closeBtn);
            }

            targetContainer.appendChild(tab);
        }
    },

    switchTab(fname, pane) {
        if (pane === 'tb') {
            this.projectFiles[this.activeTbFile] = this.tbEditor.getValue();
            this.activeTbFile = fname;
            this.tbEditor.setValue(this.projectFiles[fname]);
        } else {
            this.projectFiles[this.activeRtlFile] = this.rtlEditor.getValue();
            this.activeRtlFile = fname;
            this.rtlEditor.setValue(this.projectFiles[fname]);
        }
        this.renderTabs();
    },

    promptNewFile(pane) {
        const defaultName = (pane === 'tb') ? "tb_helper.sv" : "module.sv";
        const fname = prompt(`Enter new ${pane === 'tb' ? 'testbench' : 'design'} file name:`, defaultName);
        if (!fname || !fname.trim()) return;

        const cleanName = fname.trim();
        if (this.projectFiles[cleanName]) {
            alert(`File '${cleanName}' already exists!`);
            return;
        }

        this.projectFiles[cleanName] = `// ${cleanName}\n`;
        if (pane === 'tb') this.switchTab(cleanName, 'tb');
        else this.switchTab(cleanName, 'rtl');
    },

    deleteFile(fname, pane) {
        if (!confirm(`Delete file '${fname}'?`)) return;

        delete this.projectFiles[fname];
        if (pane === 'tb') {
            if (this.activeTbFile === fname) this.activeTbFile = "testbench.sv";
            this.tbEditor.setValue(this.projectFiles[this.activeTbFile]);
        } else {
            if (this.activeRtlFile === fname) this.activeRtlFile = "design.sv";
            this.rtlEditor.setValue(this.projectFiles[this.activeRtlFile]);
        }
        this.renderTabs();
    },

    jumpToSource(filename, lineNumber) {
        lineNumber = parseInt(lineNumber, 10);
        if (!filename || isNaN(lineNumber)) return;

        filename = filename.replace(/^.*[\\\/]/, '').trim();

        const isTbFile = filename.startsWith("tb_") || filename.startsWith("testbench");
        const targetEditor = isTbFile ? this.tbEditor : this.rtlEditor;
        const currentActiveFile = isTbFile ? this.activeTbFile : this.activeRtlFile;

        if (this.projectFiles[filename] && currentActiveFile !== filename) {
            this.switchTab(filename, isTbFile ? 'tb' : 'rtl');
        }

        targetEditor.revealLineInCenter(lineNumber);
        targetEditor.setPosition({ lineNumber: lineNumber, column: 1 });
        targetEditor.focus();

        const newDecoration = [{
            range: new monaco.Range(lineNumber, 1, lineNumber, 1),
            options: {
                isWholeLine: true,
                className: 'cas-highlight-line'
            }
        }];

        if (isTbFile) {
            this.tbDecorations = this.tbEditor.deltaDecorations(this.tbDecorations, newDecoration);
            setTimeout(() => {
                this.tbDecorations = this.tbEditor.deltaDecorations(this.tbDecorations, []);
            }, 3000);
        } else {
            this.rtlDecorations = this.rtlEditor.deltaDecorations(this.rtlDecorations, newDecoration);
            setTimeout(() => {
                this.rtlDecorations = this.rtlEditor.deltaDecorations(this.rtlDecorations, []);
            }, 3000);
        }
    },

    relayout() {
        setTimeout(() => {
            if (this.tbEditor) this.tbEditor.layout();
            if (this.rtlEditor) this.rtlEditor.layout();
        }, 50);
    }
};

window.jumpToSource = (fname, line) => window.StudioEditor.jumpToSource(fname, line);


====================================================================================================
FILE: static\js\projects.js
====================================================================================================

window.StudioProjects = {
    activeProject: null,

    newProject() {
        if (confirm("Start a new scratchpad? Any unsaved edits will be discarded.")) {
            this.activeProject = null;
            document.getElementById('active-project-label').textContent = "[Unsaved Scratchpad]";
            window.StudioWaveform.clear();

            for (const key in window.StudioEditor.projectFiles) {
                delete window.StudioEditor.projectFiles[key];
            }
            window.StudioEditor.projectFiles["testbench.sv"] = "";
            window.StudioEditor.projectFiles["design.sv"] = "";
            window.StudioEditor.activeTbFile = "testbench.sv";
            window.StudioEditor.activeRtlFile = "design.sv";
            window.StudioEditor.tbEditor.setValue("");
            window.StudioEditor.rtlEditor.setValue("");
            window.StudioEditor.renderTabs();
            this.refreshWaveDropdown([]);
        }
    },

    async save() {
        let name = this.activeProject;
        if (!name) {
            name = prompt("Enter project name:");
            if (!name) return;
        }

        window.StudioEditor.projectFiles[window.StudioEditor.activeTbFile] = window.StudioEditor.tbEditor.getValue();
        window.StudioEditor.projectFiles[window.StudioEditor.activeRtlFile] = window.StudioEditor.rtlEditor.getValue();

        const res = await fetch('/api/projects/save', {
            method: 'POST',
            headers: { 'Content-Type': 'application/json' },
            body: JSON.stringify({
                project_name: name,
                files: window.StudioEditor.projectFiles
            })
        });
        const data = await res.json();
        if (data.status === "success") {
            this.activeProject = data.project_name;
            document.getElementById('active-project-label').textContent = this.activeProject;
            alert(`Project '${this.activeProject}' (${Object.keys(window.StudioEditor.projectFiles).length} file(s)) saved.`);
        }
    },

    async openModal() {
        const modal = document.getElementById('projects-modal');
        const list = document.getElementById('projects-list');
        list.innerHTML = "Loading...";
        modal.classList.add('open');

        const res = await fetch('/api/projects');
        const data = await res.json();
        list.innerHTML = "";

        if (data.projects.length === 0) {
            list.innerHTML = "<div style='color: var(--text-muted); font-size: 12px;'>No projects saved yet.</div>";
            return;
        }

        data.projects.forEach(p => {
            const row = document.createElement('div');
            row.className = 'project-item';
            row.innerHTML = `
                <div>
                    <div class="project-item-title">${p.name}</div>
                    <div class="project-item-meta">Modified: ${p.last_modified} | ${p.vcd_count} waveform(s)</div>
                </div>
                <button class="btn btn-secondary" style="color: var(--accent-red); font-size: 11px;" onclick="StudioProjects.delete(event, '${p.name}')">Delete</button>
            `;
            row.onclick = () => this.load(p.name);
            list.appendChild(row);
        });
    },

    closeModal() {
        document.getElementById('projects-modal').classList.remove('open');
    },

    async load(name) {
        this.closeModal();
        const res = await fetch(`/api/projects/${name}`);
        const data = await res.json();

        this.activeProject = data.project_name;
        document.getElementById('active-project-label').textContent = this.activeProject;

        for (const key in window.StudioEditor.projectFiles) {
            delete window.StudioEditor.projectFiles[key];
        }
        Object.assign(window.StudioEditor.projectFiles, data.files);

        window.StudioEditor.activeTbFile = window.StudioEditor.projectFiles["testbench.sv"] ? "testbench.sv" : Object.keys(window.StudioEditor.projectFiles)[0];
        window.StudioEditor.activeRtlFile = window.StudioEditor.projectFiles["design.sv"] ? "design.sv" : Object.keys(window.StudioEditor.projectFiles)[0];

        window.StudioEditor.tbEditor.setValue(window.StudioEditor.projectFiles[window.StudioEditor.activeTbFile]);
        window.StudioEditor.rtlEditor.setValue(window.StudioEditor.projectFiles[window.StudioEditor.activeRtlFile]);
        window.StudioEditor.renderTabs();

        this.refreshWaveDropdown(data.waves);
        window.StudioWaveform.clear();

        if (data.waves.length > 0) {
            this.loadVcd(data.waves[0].filename);
        }
    },

    async delete(e, name) {
        e.stopPropagation();
        if (confirm(`Delete project '${name}' and all its waveform history?`)) {
            await fetch(`/api/projects/${name}`, { method: 'DELETE' });
            if (this.activeProject === name) this.newProject();
            this.openModal();
        }
    },

    refreshWaveDropdown(waves) {
        const sel = document.getElementById('wave-select');
        sel.innerHTML = "";
        if (waves.length === 0) {
            sel.innerHTML = '<option value="">(No traces saved)</option>';
            return;
        }
        waves.forEach(w => {
            const opt = document.createElement('option');
            opt.value = w.filename;
            opt.textContent = `${w.filename} (${w.timestamp})`;
            sel.appendChild(opt);
        });
    },

    async loadVcd(filename) {
        if (!this.activeProject) return;
        const res = await fetch(`/api/projects/${this.activeProject}/waves/${filename}`);
        const data = await res.json();
        window.StudioWaveform.activeVcdContent = data.vcd;
        window.StudioWaveform.activeVcdFilename = filename;
        document.getElementById('btn-export-vcd').disabled = false;
        window.StudioWaveform.render();
    },

    async deleteSelectedWave() {
        const sel = document.getElementById('wave-select');
        const fname = sel.value;
        if (!fname || !this.activeProject) return;
        if (confirm(`Delete waveform '${fname}'?`)) {
            await fetch(`/api/projects/${this.activeProject}/waves/${fname}`, { method: 'DELETE' });
            this.load(this.activeProject);
        }
    }
};

window.newProject = () => window.StudioProjects.newProject();
window.saveProjectPrompt = () => window.StudioProjects.save();
window.openProjectsModal = () => window.StudioProjects.openModal();
window.closeProjectsModal = () => window.StudioProjects.closeModal();
window.onSelectWaveTrace = (fname) => {
    if (!fname) window.StudioWaveform.clear();
    else window.StudioProjects.loadVcd(fname);
};
window.deleteSelectedWave = () => window.StudioProjects.deleteSelectedWave();


====================================================================================================
FILE: static\js\waveform.js

window.StudioWaveform = {
    activeVcdContent: null,
    activeVcdFilename: null,

    render() {
        const namesContainer = document.getElementById('wave-names');
        const canvas = document.getElementById('wave-canvas');
        const emptyState = document.getElementById('wave-empty');

        if (!this.activeVcdContent) {
            emptyState.style.display = "flex";
            namesContainer.innerHTML = '';
            const ctx = canvas.getContext('2d');
            ctx.clearRect(0, 0, canvas.width, canvas.height);
            return;
        }
        emptyState.style.display = "none";

        const ctx = canvas.getContext('2d');
        namesContainer.innerHTML = '';

        const lines = this.activeVcdContent.split('\n');
        const idToVar = {};
        const signals = [];
        let maxTime = 0;
        let inVars = false;

        for (let line of lines) {
            line = line.trim();
            if (line.startsWith('$var')) {
                const parts = line.split(/\s+/);
                const width = parseInt(parts[2]);
                const id = parts[3];
                const name = parts[4];
                const sig = { id, name, width, changes: [] };
                idToVar[id] = sig;
                signals.push(sig);
            } else if (line.startsWith('$enddefinitions')) {
                inVars = true;
            } else if (inVars && line.startsWith('#')) {
                const t = parseInt(line.substring(1));
                if (t > maxTime) maxTime = t;
            }
        }

        if (maxTime === 0) maxTime = 50;

        let curTime = 0;
        for (let line of lines) {
            line = line.trim();
            if (!line || line.startsWith('$')) continue;
            if (line.startsWith('#')) {
                curTime = parseInt(line.substring(1));
                continue;
            }

            if (line.startsWith('b')) {
                const parts = line.substring(1).split(/\s+/);
                const val = parts[0];
                const id = parts[1];
                if (idToVar[id]) {
                    const isX = val.includes('x') || val.includes('z');
                    idToVar[id].changes.push({ time: curTime, val: isX ? 'x' : parseInt(val, 2), raw: val });
                }
            } else {
                const charVal = line[0].toLowerCase();
                const id = line.substring(1);
                if (idToVar[id]) {
                    idToVar[id].changes.push({ time: curTime, val: charVal });
                }
            }
        }

        const rowHeight = 36;
        const timeHeaderHeight = 28;
        const timeStepPx = 14;
        const totalWidth = Math.max(800, (maxTime + 10) * timeStepPx);
        const totalHeight = timeHeaderHeight + (signals.length * rowHeight);

        canvas.width = totalWidth;
        canvas.height = totalHeight;

        signals.forEach(s => {
            const div = document.createElement('div');
            div.className = 'wave-sig-row';
            div.innerHTML = `<span>${s.name}</span><span class="wave-sig-type">${s.width > 1 ? '[' + (s.width-1) + ':0]' : 'wire'}</span>`;
            namesContainer.appendChild(div);
        });

        ctx.fillStyle = '#0b0c0e';
        ctx.fillRect(0, 0, totalWidth, totalHeight);

        ctx.fillStyle = '#16171d';
        ctx.fillRect(0, 0, totalWidth, timeHeaderHeight);
        ctx.strokeStyle = '#272a33';
        ctx.lineWidth = 1;
        ctx.beginPath();
        ctx.moveTo(0, timeHeaderHeight);
        ctx.lineTo(totalWidth, timeHeaderHeight);
        ctx.stroke();

        ctx.font = '10px JetBrains Mono';
        ctx.fillStyle = '#8b949e';

        for (let t = 0; t <= maxTime + 5; t += 5) {
            const x = t * timeStepPx;
            ctx.beginPath();
            ctx.moveTo(x, timeHeaderHeight - 6);
            ctx.lineTo(x, totalHeight);
            ctx.strokeStyle = (t % 10 === 0) ? '#272a33' : '#1a1b22';
            ctx.stroke();
            ctx.fillText(t + 'ns', x + 3, timeHeaderHeight - 8);
        }

        signals.forEach((sig, idx) => {
            const yTop = timeHeaderHeight + (idx * rowHeight);
            const yMid = yTop + (rowHeight / 2);
            const yHigh = yTop + 7;
            const yLow = yTop + rowHeight - 7;

            ctx.strokeStyle = '#181920';
            ctx.beginPath();
            ctx.moveTo(0, yTop + rowHeight);
            ctx.lineTo(totalWidth, yTop + rowHeight);
            ctx.stroke();

            if (sig.changes.length === 0) return;

            if (sig.width === 1) {
                for (let i = 0; i < sig.changes.length; i++) {
                    const c = sig.changes[i];
                    const nextTime = (i + 1 < sig.changes.length) ? sig.changes[i+1].time : (maxTime + 5);
                    const xStart = c.time * timeStepPx;
                    const xEnd = nextTime * timeStepPx;

                    ctx.lineWidth = 2;
                    if (c.val === 'x' || c.val === 'z') {
                        ctx.strokeStyle = '#ef4444';
                        ctx.beginPath();
                        ctx.moveTo(xStart, yMid);
                        ctx.lineTo(xEnd, yMid);
                        ctx.stroke();
                    } else {
                        ctx.strokeStyle = '#10b981';
                        const yVal = (c.val === '1' || c.val === 1) ? yHigh : yLow;
                        ctx.beginPath();
                        ctx.moveTo(xStart, yVal);
                        ctx.lineTo(xEnd, yVal);
                        ctx.stroke();
                    }

                    if (i + 1 < sig.changes.length) {
                        const nextVal = sig.changes[i+1].val;
                        const yCur = (c.val === 'x' || c.val === 'z') ? yMid : ((c.val === '1' || c.val === 1) ? yHigh : yLow);
                        const yNext = (nextVal === 'x' || nextVal === 'z') ? yMid : ((nextVal === '1' || nextVal === 1) ? yHigh : yLow);
                        ctx.strokeStyle = (nextVal === 'x' || c.val === 'x') ? '#ef4444' : '#10b981';
                        ctx.beginPath();
                        ctx.moveTo(xEnd, yCur);
                        ctx.lineTo(xEnd, yNext);
                        ctx.stroke();
                    }
                }
            } else {
                for (let i = 0; i < sig.changes.length; i++) {
                    const c = sig.changes[i];
                    const nextTime = (i + 1 < sig.changes.length) ? sig.changes[i+1].time : (maxTime + 5);
                    const xStart = c.time * timeStepPx;
                    const xEnd = nextTime * timeStepPx;
                    const boxW = xEnd - xStart;

                    if (boxW <= 0) continue;

                    const isX = (c.val === 'x');
                    ctx.strokeStyle = isX ? '#ef4444' : '#38bdf8';
                    ctx.fillStyle = isX ? '#450a0a' : '#1e293b';
                    ctx.lineWidth = 1.5;

                    ctx.beginPath();
                    ctx.moveTo(xStart + 3, yHigh);
                    ctx.lineTo(xEnd - 3, yHigh);
                    ctx.lineTo(xEnd, yMid);
                    ctx.lineTo(xEnd - 3, yLow);
                    ctx.lineTo(xStart + 3, yLow);
                    ctx.lineTo(xStart, yMid);
                    ctx.closePath();
                    ctx.fill();
                    ctx.stroke();

                    ctx.fillStyle = isX ? '#fca5a5' : '#e2e8f0';
                    ctx.font = '11px JetBrains Mono';
                    const label = isX ? "X" : ("0x" + c.val.toString(16).toUpperCase());
                    if (boxW > 25) ctx.fillText(label, xStart + 8, yMid + 4);
                }
            }
        });
    },

    clear() {
        this.activeVcdContent = null;
        this.activeVcdFilename = null;
        document.getElementById('btn-export-vcd').disabled = true;
        document.getElementById('wave-empty').style.display = "flex";
        document.getElementById('wave-names').innerHTML = "";
        const canvas = document.getElementById('wave-canvas');
        const ctx = canvas.getContext('2d');
        ctx.clearRect(0, 0, canvas.width, canvas.height);
    },

    download() {
        if (!this.activeVcdContent) return;
        const blob = new Blob([this.activeVcdContent], { type: 'text/plain' });
        const url = URL.createObjectURL(blob);
        const a = document.createElement('a');
        a.href = url;
        a.download = this.activeVcdFilename || "trace.vcd";
        document.body.appendChild(a);
        a.click();
        document.body.removeChild(a);
        URL.revokeObjectURL(url);
    }
};

window.downloadActiveVcd = () => window.StudioWaveform.download();

====================================================================================================
FILE: static\vcd\js\vcd_parser.js

window.VcdParser = {
    parse(vcdText) {
        if (!vcdText || typeof vcdText !== 'string') {
            return { signals: {}, signalList: [], maxTime: 0, timescale: '1ns' };
        }

        const lines = vcdText.split(/\r?\n/);
        const idToSignal = {};
        const signals = {};
        const scopeStack = [];
        let inDefinitions = true;
        let currentTime = 0;
        let maxTime = 0;
        let timescale = '1ns';

        for (let i = 0; i < lines.length; i++) {
            let line = lines[i].trim();
            if (!line) continue;

            if (inDefinitions) {
                if (line.startsWith('$timescale')) {
                    // Extract timescale until $end
                    let tsContent = line;
                    while (!tsContent.includes('$end') && i + 1 < lines.length) {
                        i++;
                        tsContent += ' ' + lines[i].trim();
                    }
                    const m = tsContent.match(/\$timescale\s+([^\$]+)\s*\$end/);
                    if (m) timescale = m[1].trim();
                    continue;
                }

                if (line.startsWith('$scope')) {
                    const tokens = line.split(/\s+/);
                    if (tokens.length >= 3) {
                        scopeStack.push(tokens[2]);
                    }
                    continue;
                }

                if (line.startsWith('$upscope')) {
                    if (scopeStack.length > 0) scopeStack.pop();
                    continue;
                }

                if (line.startsWith('$var')) {
                    const tokens = line.split(/\s+/);
                    if (tokens.length >= 5) {
                        const type = tokens[1];
                        const size = parseInt(tokens[2], 10) || 1;
                        const id = tokens[3];
                        const rawName = tokens[4];

                        // Build scoped hierarchy name
                        const fullPath = scopeStack.length > 0 
                            ? (scopeStack.join('.') + '.' + rawName)
                            : rawName;

                        // Create clean display leaf name
                        const cleanLeaf = rawName;

                        const sigObj = {
                            id: id,
                            name: cleanLeaf,
                            fullName: fullPath,
                            size: size,
                            type: type,
                            changes: []
                        };

                        idToSignal[id] = sigObj;
                        signals[fullPath] = sigObj;
                    }
                    continue;
                }

                if (line.startsWith('$enddefinitions')) {
                    inDefinitions = false;
                    continue;
                }
            } else {
                // Value changes and simulation timestamps
                if (line.startsWith('#')) {
                    currentTime = parseInt(line.substring(1), 10) || 0;
                    if (currentTime > maxTime) maxTime = currentTime;
                    continue;
                }

                if (line.startsWith('$dumpvars') || line.startsWith('$end')) {
                    continue;
                }

                // Vector value change: b1010 identifier or bX identifier
                if (line.startsWith('b') || line.startsWith('B')) {
                    const tokens = line.substring(1).trim().split(/\s+/);
                    if (tokens.length >= 2) {
                        const val = tokens[0].toLowerCase();
                        const id = tokens[1];
                        if (idToSignal[id]) {
                            idToSignal[id].changes.push({ time: currentTime, value: val });
                        }
                    }
                    continue;
                }

                // Single-bit value change: 0identifier or 1identifier or xidentifier
                const bitVal = line[0].toLowerCase();
                const id = line.substring(1).trim();
                if (idToSignal[id]) {
                    idToSignal[id].changes.push({ time: currentTime, value: bitVal });
                }
            }
        }

        const signalList = Object.values(signals);

        return {
            signals: signals,
            signalList: signalList,
            maxTime: maxTime,
            timescale: timescale
        };
    }
};


====================================================================================================
FILE: static\vcd\js\vcd_viewer.js
====================================================================================================

window.VcdViewer = {
    canvas: null,
    ctx: null,
    activeVcdContent: null,
    activeVcdFilename: null,

    parsedData: null,
    activeSignalKeys: [], // Full scoped names currently displayed

    // View metrics
    timeStepPx: 14,
    rowHeight: 34,
    timeHeaderHeight: 28,
    cursorTime: 0,

    init() {
        this.canvas = document.getElementById('vcd-canvas');
        if (this.canvas) {
            this.ctx = this.canvas.getContext('2d');
            this.setupEvents();
        }
    },

    setupEvents() {
        if (!this.canvas) return;

        this.canvas.addEventListener('click', (e) => {
            const rect = this.canvas.getBoundingClientRect();
            const clickX = e.clientX - rect.left;
            const t = Math.max(0, Math.round(clickX / this.timeStepPx));
            this.setCursorTime(t);
        });

        const viewport = document.getElementById('vcd-viewport-pane');
        const headerPane = document.getElementById('vcd-names-pane');
        if (viewport && headerPane) {
            viewport.addEventListener('scroll', () => {
                headerPane.scrollTop = viewport.scrollTop;
            });
        }
    },

    setCursorTime(t) {
        this.cursorTime = t;
        const lbl = document.getElementById('vcd-marker-time');
        if (lbl) lbl.textContent = `${t} ns`;
        this.render();
    },

    zoomIn() {
        this.timeStepPx = Math.min(80, this.timeStepPx + 4);
        this.render();
    },

    zoomOut() {
        this.timeStepPx = Math.max(4, this.timeStepPx - 4);
        this.render();
    },

    stepNext() {
        if (!this.parsedData) return;
        let nextTime = Infinity;
        this.activeSignalKeys.forEach(k => {
            const sig = this.parsedData.signals[k];
            if (sig) {
                sig.changes.forEach(c => {
                    if (c.time > this.cursorTime && c.time < nextTime) {
                        nextTime = c.time;
                    }
                });
            }
        });
        if (nextTime !== Infinity) this.setCursorTime(nextTime);
    },

    stepPrev() {
        if (!this.parsedData) return;
        let prevTime = -1;
        this.activeSignalKeys.forEach(k => {
            const sig = this.parsedData.signals[k];
            if (sig) {
                sig.changes.forEach(c => {
                    if (c.time < this.cursorTime && c.time > prevTime) {
                        prevTime = c.time;
                    }
                });
            }
        });
        if (prevTime !== -1) this.setCursorTime(prevTime);
    },

    loadTrace(vcdText, filename = "trace.vcd") {
        this.activeVcdContent = vcdText;
        this.activeVcdFilename = filename;
        this.parsedData = window.VcdParser.parse(vcdText);

        // Preload up to 16 signals on initial trace load
        this.activeSignalKeys = this.parsedData.signalList.slice(0, 16).map(s => s.fullName);
        this.cursorTime = 0;

        const tsLbl = document.getElementById('vcd-timescale-lbl');
        if (tsLbl && this.parsedData.timescale) {
            tsLbl.textContent = this.parsedData.timescale;
        }

        const exportBtn = document.getElementById('btn-export-vcd');
        if (exportBtn) exportBtn.disabled = false;

        this.render();
    },

    clear() {
        this.activeVcdContent = null;
        this.activeVcdFilename = null;
        this.parsedData = null;
        this.activeSignalKeys = [];
        const exportBtn = document.getElementById('btn-export-vcd');
        if (exportBtn) exportBtn.disabled = true;
        this.render();
    },

    getValueAtTime(sigObj, time) {
        if (!sigObj || !sigObj.changes || sigObj.changes.length === 0) return 'x';
        let val = 'x';
        for (let i = 0; i < sigObj.changes.length; i++) {
            if (sigObj.changes[i].time <= time) {
                val = sigObj.changes[i].value;
            } else {
                break;
            }
        }
        return val;
    },

    render() {
        if (!this.canvas) this.canvas = document.getElementById('vcd-canvas');
        if (!this.canvas) return;
        this.ctx = this.canvas.getContext('2d');

        const namesPane = document.getElementById('vcd-names-pane');
        const emptyState = document.getElementById('vcd-empty-state');

        if (!this.activeVcdContent || !this.parsedData) {
            if (emptyState) emptyState.style.display = 'flex';
            if (namesPane) namesPane.innerHTML = '';
            this.ctx.clearRect(0, 0, this.canvas.width, this.canvas.height);
            return;
        }

        if (emptyState) emptyState.style.display = 'none';

        const maxTime = Math.max(10, this.parsedData.maxTime);
        const totalW = Math.max(900, (maxTime + 10) * this.timeStepPx);
        const totalH = this.timeHeaderHeight + (this.activeSignalKeys.length * this.rowHeight);

        this.canvas.width = totalW;
        this.canvas.height = totalH;

        // 1. Render Left Names Pane
        if (namesPane) {
            namesPane.innerHTML = `
                <div class="vcd-header-row">
                    <span>SIGNAL</span>
                    <span>VAL @ T</span>
                </div>
            `;

            this.activeSignalKeys.forEach(k => {
                const sig = this.parsedData.signals[k];
                if (!sig) return;

                const isBus = sig.size > 1;
                const rawVal = this.getValueAtTime(sig, this.cursorTime);

                const row = document.createElement('div');
                row.className = 'vcd-sig-row';
                row.innerHTML = `
                    <div style="display: flex; align-items: center; overflow: hidden; text-overflow: ellipsis; white-space: nowrap;" title="${sig.fullName}">
                        <span style="color: ${isBus ? '#38bdf8' : '#34d399'}; font-weight: 600;">${sig.name}</span>
                        <span class="vcd-sig-meta">${isBus ? '[' + (sig.size-1) + ':0]' : 'wire'}</span>
                    </div>
                    <span style="font-size: 10px; font-weight: 600; color: #fbbf24;">${rawVal}</span>
                `;
                namesPane.appendChild(row);
            });
        }

        // 2. Clear Canvas & Draw Time Ruler
        this.ctx.fillStyle = '#0b0c0e';
        this.ctx.fillRect(0, 0, totalW, totalH);

        this.ctx.fillStyle = '#14161b';
        this.ctx.fillRect(0, 0, totalW, this.timeHeaderHeight);
        this.ctx.strokeStyle = '#282b36';
        this.ctx.lineWidth = 1;
        this.ctx.beginPath();
        this.ctx.moveTo(0, this.timeHeaderHeight);
        this.ctx.lineTo(totalW, this.timeHeaderHeight);
        this.ctx.stroke();

        this.ctx.font = '10px JetBrains Mono';
        this.ctx.fillStyle = '#64748b';
        for (let t = 0; t <= maxTime + 10; t += 5) {
            const x = t * this.timeStepPx;
            this.ctx.beginPath();
            this.ctx.moveTo(x, this.timeHeaderHeight);
            this.ctx.lineTo(x, totalH);
            this.ctx.strokeStyle = (t % 10 === 0) ? '#1f232d' : '#14171e';
            this.ctx.stroke();

            if (t % 10 === 0) {
                this.ctx.fillText(`${t}ns`, x + 3, this.timeHeaderHeight - 8);
            }
        }

        // 3. Draw Signal Lanes
        this.activeSignalKeys.forEach((k, rowIdx) => {
            const sig = this.parsedData.signals[k];
            if (!sig) return;

            const isBus = sig.size > 1;
            const yTop = this.timeHeaderHeight + (rowIdx * this.rowHeight);
            const yMid = yTop + (this.rowHeight / 2);
            const yHigh = yTop + 7;
            const yLow = yTop + this.rowHeight - 7;

            // Lane Divider
            this.ctx.strokeStyle = '#181a20';
            this.ctx.beginPath();
            this.ctx.moveTo(0, yTop + this.rowHeight);
            this.ctx.lineTo(totalW, yTop + this.rowHeight);
            this.ctx.stroke();

            const changes = sig.changes || [];
            if (changes.length === 0) return;

            if (!isBus) {
                // 1-Bit Digital Wire
                for (let i = 0; i < changes.length; i++) {
                    const ev = changes[i];
                    const nextT = (i + 1 < changes.length) ? changes[i + 1].time : (maxTime + 10);
                    const xStart = ev.time * this.timeStepPx;
                    const xEnd = nextT * this.timeStepPx;

                    const isHigh = (ev.value === '1');
                    const isX = (ev.value === 'x' || ev.value === 'z');

                    this.ctx.lineWidth = 1.8;
                    if (isX) {
                        this.ctx.strokeStyle = '#f43f5e';
                        this.ctx.beginPath();
                        this.ctx.moveTo(xStart, yMid);
                        this.ctx.lineTo(xEnd, yMid);
                        this.ctx.stroke();
                    } else {
                        this.ctx.strokeStyle = '#34d399';
                        const yVal = isHigh ? yHigh : yLow;
                        this.ctx.beginPath();
                        this.ctx.moveTo(xStart, yVal);
                        this.ctx.lineTo(xEnd, yVal);
                        this.ctx.stroke();
                    }

                    if (i + 1 < changes.length) {
                        const nextEv = changes[i + 1];
                        const nextHigh = (nextEv.value === '1');
                        const nextX = (nextEv.value === 'x' || nextEv.value === 'z');

                        const yCur = isX ? yMid : (isHigh ? yHigh : yLow);
                        const yNext = nextX ? yMid : (nextHigh ? yHigh : yLow);

                        this.ctx.strokeStyle = (isX || nextX) ? '#f43f5e' : '#34d399';
                        this.ctx.beginPath();
                        this.ctx.moveTo(xEnd, yCur);
                        this.ctx.lineTo(xEnd, yNext);
                        this.ctx.stroke();
                    }
                }
            } else {
                // Multi-Bit Hex/Bin Bus
                for (let i = 0; i < changes.length; i++) {
                    const ev = changes[i];
                    const nextT = (i + 1 < changes.length) ? changes[i + 1].time : (maxTime + 10);
                    const xStart = ev.time * this.timeStepPx;
                    const xEnd = nextT * this.timeStepPx;
                    const boxW = xEnd - xStart;

                    if (boxW <= 0) continue;

                    const isX = ev.value.includes('x') || ev.value.includes('z');
                    this.ctx.strokeStyle = isX ? '#f43f5e' : '#38bdf8';
                    this.ctx.fillStyle = isX ? 'rgba(244, 63, 94, 0.15)' : 'rgba(56, 189, 248, 0.12)';
                    this.ctx.lineWidth = 1.5;

                    this.ctx.beginPath();
                    this.ctx.moveTo(xStart + 3, yHigh);
                    this.ctx.lineTo(xEnd - 3, yHigh);
                    this.ctx.lineTo(xEnd, yMid);
                    this.ctx.lineTo(xEnd - 3, yLow);
                    this.ctx.lineTo(xStart + 3, yLow);
                    this.ctx.lineTo(xStart, yMid);
                    this.ctx.closePath();
                    this.ctx.fill();
                    this.ctx.stroke();

                    this.ctx.fillStyle = isX ? '#fda4af' : '#e2e8f0';
                    this.ctx.font = '10.5px JetBrains Mono';
                    if (boxW > 24) {
                        this.ctx.fillText(ev.value, xStart + 6, yMid + 3.5);
                    }
                }
            }
        });

        // 4. Yellow Interactive Cursor
        const markerX = this.cursorTime * this.timeStepPx;
        this.ctx.strokeStyle = '#fbbf24';
        this.ctx.lineWidth = 1.5;
        this.ctx.beginPath();
        this.ctx.moveTo(markerX, 0);
        this.ctx.lineTo(markerX, totalH);
        this.ctx.stroke();
    }
};

window.downloadActiveVcd = () => {
    if (!window.VcdViewer.activeVcdContent) return;
    const blob = new Blob([window.VcdViewer.activeVcdContent], { type: 'text/plain' });
    const url = URL.createObjectURL(blob);
    const a = document.createElement('a');
    a.href = url;
    a.download = window.VcdViewer.activeVcdFilename || 'trace.vcd';
    a.click();
    URL.revokeObjectURL(url);
};


====================================================================================================
FILE: VERSIONS.md
====================================================================================================

# CAS Simulator Release Notes & Roadmap

## Version History

### `v0.9.3` - Gate Primitives, Positional Port Slices & Continuous Monitoring
* **Built-in Gate Primitives:** Evaluates IEEE 1800 primitives (`and`, `or`, `xor`, `nand`, `nor`, `xnor`, `not`, `buf`) with multi-input fan-in directly within the combinational delta pass.
* **Positional Port Slice Binding:** Submodule instantiation supports positional arguments mapped directly to bus slices (e.g., `full_adder fa0 (sum[0], c[0], a[0], b[0], cin);`).
* **Dynamic Sensitivity Monitoring (`$monitor`):** Automatic callback mechanism tracking sensitivity variable updates across discrete-event steps with formatted console output.
* **Non-ANSI Header Support:** Lexer and parser support for legacy Verilog-1995/2001 port declarations (`module m (a, b); input a; output b;`).
* **Subroutine Task Execution:** User-defined procedural `task` blocks with variable parameters and prefix delays (`#delay`).

---

### `v0.9.0` - Dataflow Modeling & Unpacked Memory Arrays
* **Continuous `assign` Dataflow:** Module-level continuous assignments evaluated concurrently in the discrete-event delta-cycle loop.
* **1D Memory Arrays:** Native syntax support for `logic [W-1:0] mem [0:D-1];` arrays, including asynchronous indexed reads (`mem[addr]`) and synchronous non-blocking writes (`mem[addr] <= data`).
* **Memory Array Unknown Power-On State:** All unwritten memory words initialize to 4-state unknown (`X`) vectors.

---

### `v0.8.0` - Control Flow Selection & Vector Part-Select Slicing
* **Control Selection (`case` / `endcase`):** Multi-way branch selection supporting exact value matching, default fallbacks, and multi-statement procedural blocks.
* **Bit-Select & Part-Select Extraction:** Vector scalar bit selection (`signal[i]`) and variable part-select slicing (`signal[msb:lsb]`) with 4-state logic alignment.

---

### `v0.7.0` - Extended ALU Operators & Recursive Hierarchical Elaboration
* **Comprehensive Operator Set:** Added logical shifts (`<<`, `>>`), relational gates (`<`, `<=`, `>`, `>=`), equality gates (`==`, `!=`), bitwise logic (`&`, `|`, `^`, `~`), and ternary conditional evaluation (`cond ? a : b`).
* **Arbitrary-Depth Recursive Elaboration:** Recursive AST traversal resolving nested submodule instantiations (`top -> dut -> submod -> ...`) and binding cross-boundary net slices.

---

### `v0.6.0` - Dual-Rail 4-State Logic Engine
* **Dual-Word Representation (`0, 1, X, Z`):** Implemented dual 64-bit integer masks (`aval` data word, `bval` unknown mask) across all nets and variables.
* **IEEE 1800 Unknown Propagation:** Bitwise logic gates and arithmetic operations correctly handle unknown inputs (`0 & X = 0`, `1 | X = 1`, `X ^ X = X`).
* **Asynchronous Reset Fall-Through:** Detects 4-state unknown transitions to definite states (`X -> 0` safely activates `negedge`).

---

### `v0.5.1` - Workspace Engine & VCD Timeline Synchronization
* **Project Directory Workspaces:** Directory isolation (`projects/<name>/`) storing RTL designs, testbenches, waveforms, and configuration metadata.
* **Persistent Waveform Archive:** Multi-run VCD history tracking per workspace with deletion and selection controls.

---

### `v0.5.0` - Native VCD Waveform Generation & Digital Canvas
* **Value Change Dump Generator:** High-throughput binary-to-ASCII dumper generating IEEE 1364/1800 `.vcd` files via `$dumpfile` and `$dumpvars`.
* **HTML5 Waveform Viewer:** Digital timing canvas visualizing clocks, single-bit logic transitions, and multi-bit hexadecimal buses.

---

### `v0.4.0` - Stratified IEEE 1800 Event Regions
* **Active vs. NBA Event Scheduling:** Two-phase execution buffering non-blocking assignments (`<=`) and committing register updates synchronously before combinational delta passes.
* **Edge Sensitivity Engine:** Dynamic edge tracking (`is_posedge()`, `is_negedge()`) for sequential processes (`always_ff` / `always @(posedge clk)`).

---

### `v0.3.0` - Discrete-Event Time Wheel
* **Min-Heap Event Scheduler:** Priority-queue discrete-event engine dispatching timed events across `#delay` intervals.
* **System Call `$time`:** Native simulation timestamp queries.

---

### `v0.2.0` - Hierarchical Module Instantiation
* Named port binding (`.port(wire)`) and submodule instantiation.

---

### `v0.1.0` - Core Discrete-Event Kernel
* Standalone AST, delta-cycle convergence loop, primitive netlist representation, and foundational system tasks (`$display`, `$finish`).

---

## Roadmap

| Version | Focus Feature Set | Status |
| :--- | :--- | :--- |
| **`v0.1.0`** | AST, netlist, flat delta-cycle loop, `$display`, `$finish` | **Released** |
| **`v0.2.0`** | Hierarchical module instantiation, named port mapping | **Released** |
| **`v0.3.0`** | Priority-queue event scheduler, `#delay`, `$time` | **Released** |
| **`v0.4.0`** | Sequential logic, IEEE 1800 Active vs. NBA, `always_ff @(posedge/negedge)` | **Released** |
| **`v0.5.0`** | Native VCD generation (`$dumpfile`, `$dumpvars`), canvas waveform engine | **Released** |
| **`v0.6.0`** | 4-State Logic Engine (`0, 1, X, Z`), dual-rail bit vectors | **Released** |
| **`v0.7.0`** | Extended ALU operators, recursive elaboration, regression suite | **Released** |
| **`v0.8.0`** | Control flow (`case`), vector slicing (`wire[idx]`, `wire[msb:lsb]`) | **Released** |
| **`v0.9.0`** | Continuous `assign`, 1D unpacked memory arrays | **Released** |
| **`v0.9.3`** | Gate primitives (`and`, `or`...), positional port slices, `$monitor` | **Released** |
| **`v0.9.4`** | Parameterized module instantiations, localparam overrides | **Active** |
| **`v1.0.0`** | RISC-V Verification: Cycle-accurate RV32I execution | *Next* |
